The markdown source to parse.\n1323:+ * @returns The parsed document and its node-identity span map.\n1345:+ * A half-open region of the ORIGINAL markdown string, in UTF-16 code units -\n1346:+ * `start` inclusive, `end` exclusive. The provenance a parse records for a node and\n1347:+ * {@link MarkdownInterface.span} reads back.\n1349:+ * @remarks\n1350:+ * The coordinates address the string the workspace was constructed from, never the\n1351:+ * line text a later phase walks, so `markdown.slice(span.start, span.end)` returns\n1352:+ * the source the node was parsed from. The region's length is `end - start`; no\n1353:+ * length member exists to drift from the two offsets.\n1363:+ * One run of a {@link MarkdownSource} - the mapping from a stretch of derived text\n1364:+ * back to the region of the ORIGINAL markdown string it was taken from.\n1366:+ * @remarks\n1367:+ * `offset` addresses {@link MarkdownSource.text}; `start` and `end` address the\n1368:+ * original string. The run's length is `end - start` in both, so no length member\n1369:+ * exists, and a position `p` inside the run projects back to `start + (p - offset)`.\n1370:+ * Every strip, trim, and join the block phase performs is affine at this\n1371:+ * granularity, which is what lets a derived offset carry an original coordinate.\n1383:+ * A piece of derived markdown text paired with the runs mapping it back to the\n1384:+ * original string - what `splitLines` returns per line, so every phase downstream of\n1385:+ * it keeps original coordinates instead of reconstructing them from node values.\n1387:+ * @remarks\n1388:+ * `text` is the line a parser reads: its terminator, `>` quote marker, or leading\n1389:+ * indent already removed. `segments` cover `text` in ascending `offset` order, one\n1390:+ * run per contiguous stretch of the original; a piece assembled from separate\n1391:+ * stretches carries one segment per stretch.\n1408:+ * A parsed document paired with the {@link MarkdownSpan} of each of its nodes - what\n1409:+ * `parseProvenance` returns, and what `parseDocument` projects the document out of.\n1411:+ * @remarks\n1412:+ * `spans` is keyed by node identity, so it addresses the nodes of THAT document and\n1413:+ * no other; a node built from separate regions of the source, or from none, is\n1414:+ * absent rather than mapped to a placeholder region. Destructure it as\n1415:+ * `const [document, spans] = parseProvenance(markdown)`.\n1423:+ * A rewritten value paired with the input node each rewritten node came from - what\n1424:+ * `rewriteDocument` returns, so provenance survives a rewrite instead of ending at\n1425:+ * it. `T` is the rewritten value: the document for a whole-document rewrite.\n1427:+ * @remarks\n1428:+ * `derivations` is keyed by the nodes of the OUTPUT and read against the source\n1429:+ * workspace's own spans:\n1431:+ * - a node mapped to an input node takes that input's span;\n1432:+ * - a node mapped to `undefined` was produced from separate sources - a joined text\n1433:+ *   run, a synthesized paragraph - so no single input covers it and it has no span;\n1434:+ * - an absent entry means the output node kept its own identity, unchanged by the\n1435:+ *   rewrite, so the span it already had still stands.\n1453:+ * - **The surface.** `document` (the AST root), `walk` (the deep traversal), `find` /\n1454:+ *   `filter` / `reduce` (queries built on `walk`), `span` (the region of the original\n1455:+ *   markdown a node was parsed from), `map` (the bottom-up rewrite), `fold` (the\n1456:+ *   total catamorphism), and `stream` (the shallow, backpressured top-level source)."}]},"uuid":"9c30a299-56dc-4602-b549-26b02bbfe6a1","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:08:06.719Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"03c8abf8-54ca-4e49-aa54-0e3e8d5cb5e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}

{"parentUuid":"69a9aa25-760b-465d-83fb-531488b1f333","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"087400fb-8173-483a-91fe-ccaa67ffaa7b","agentId":"ae24d09dc33db3b10","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HjBW66AKNYdpSp9su43gbj","type":"tool_result","content":"388\t * replacement (the same node, unchanged, or a new node).\n389\t */\n390\texport type MarkdownRewriteHandler = (node: MarkdownNode) => MarkdownNode\n391\t\n392\t/**\n393\t * A parsed document paired with the {@link MarkdownSpan} of each of its nodes - what\n394\t * `parseProvenance` returns, and what `parseDocument` projects the document out of.\n395\t *\n396\t * @remarks\n397\t * `spans` is keyed by node identity, so it addresses the nodes of THAT document and\n398\t * no other; a node built from separate regions of the source, or from none, is\n399\t * absent rather than mapped to a placeholder region. Destructure it as\n400\t * `const [document, spans] = parseProvenance(markdown)`.\n401\t */\n402\texport type MarkdownParseResult = readonly [\n403\t\tdocument: MarkdownDocument,\n404\t\tspans: ReadonlyMap<MarkdownNode, MarkdownSpan>,\n405\t]\n406\t\n407\t/**\n408\t * A rewritten value paired with the input node each rewritten node came from - what\n409\t * `rewriteDocument` returns, so provenance survives a rewrite instead of ending at\n410\t * it. `T` is the rewritten value: the document for a whole-document rewrite.\n411\t *\n412\t * @remarks\n413\t * `derivations` is keyed by the nodes of the OUTPUT and read against the source\n414\t * handle's own spans:\n415\t *\n416\t * - a node mapped to an input node takes that input's span;\n417\t * - a node mapped to `undefined` was produced from separate sources - a joined text\n418\t *   run, a synthesized paragraph - so no single input covers it and it has no span;\n419\t * - an absent entry means the output node kept its own identity, unchanged by the\n420\t *   rewrite, so the span it already had still stands.\n421\t */\n422\texport type MarkdownDerivation<T> = readonly [\n423\t\tvalue: T,\n424\t\tderivations: ReadonlyMap<MarkdownNode, MarkdownNode | undefined>,\n425\t]\n426\t\n427\t/**\n428\t * A stateful, parsed markdown document: the typed {@link MarkdownDocument} AST plus\n429\t * the query, rewrite, and fold operations over it.\n430\t *\n431\t * @remarks\n432\t * - **Immutable.** {@link MarkdownInterface.map} never mutates the stored AST - it\n433\t *   returns a NEW {@link MarkdownInterface} instance; the document root invariant\n434\t *   (`element: 'document'`) always holds.\n435\t * - **Traversal order.** `walk` / `find` / `filter` / `reduce` walk the AST\n436\t *   depth-first, pre-order, root-inclusive; `stream` is shallow - only the\n437\t *   document's direct block children.\n438\t * - **`stream`.** Returns a web-standard {@link ReadableStream} over the top-level\n439\t *   blocks - a fresh, pull-based source per call: exactly one block is enqueued per\n440\t *   `pull`, so a slow consumer's backpressure is respected and no work happens ahead\n441\t *   of demand. Cancellable via the returned stream's own `cancel()`, async-iterable\n442\t *   wherever the platform supports it (Node, Deno, and browsers that ship the\n443\t *   proposal), and pipeable through any {@link TransformStream} / {@link WritableStream}.\n444\t * - **The surface.** `document` (the AST root), `walk` (the deep traversal), `find` /\n445\t *   `filter` / `reduce` (queries built on `walk`), `span` (the region of the original\n446\t *   markdown a node was parsed from), `map` (the bottom-up rewrite), `fold` (the\n447\t *   total catamorphism), and `stream` (the shallow, backpressured top-level source).\n448\t */\n449\texport interface MarkdownInterface {\n450\t\t/** The stored {@link MarkdownDocument} AST root. */\n451\t\treadonly document: MarkdownDocument\n452\t\t/**\n453\t\t * THE deep traversal - a lazy, depth-first, pre-order, root-inclusive\n454\t\t * {@link Generator} over every {@link MarkdownNode} in the document. The sync\n455\t\t * `for (const node of markdown.walk())` surface is also consumable by\n456\t\t * `for await (const node of markdown.walk())` (JavaScript accepts a sync\n457\t\t * iterable in a `for await`), so async pipelines need no separate iterator.\n458\t\t * Contrast with {@link stream}: `walk` is deep, every-node, and sync; `stream`\n459\t\t * is shallow (top-level blocks only) and backpressure-respecting.\n460\t\t */\n461\t\twalk(): Generator<MarkdownNode>\n462\t\t/** Finds the first node (depth-first, pre-order) narrowed by a type guard. */\n463\t\tfind<T extends MarkdownNode>(guard: (node: MarkdownNode) => node is T): T | undefined\n464\t\t/** Finds the first node (depth-first, pre-order) matching a predicate. */\n465\t\tfind(predicate: (node: MarkdownNode) => boolean): MarkdownNode | undefined\n466\t\t/** Collects every node (depth-first, pre-order) narrowed by a type guard. */\n467\t\tfilter<T extends MarkdownNode>(guard: (node: MarkdownNode) => node is T): readonly T[]\n468\t\t/** Collects every node (depth-first, pre-order) matching a predicate. */\n469\t\tfilter(predicate: (node: MarkdownNode) => boolean): readonly MarkdownNode[]\n470\t\t/**\n471\t\t * Reads the region of the original markdown string a node was parsed from.\n472\t\t *\n473\t\t * @param node - A node of this handle's document.\n474\t\t * @returns A fresh {@link MarkdownSpan}, or `undefined` when the node has no single\n475\t\t * source region.\n476\t\t *\n477\t\t * @remarks\n478\t\t * Provenance is per handle and per node identity, so a node reports a region\n479\t\t * only where THIS handle parsed it from a string. A handle constructed from\n480\t\t * an adopted {@link MarkdownDocument} reports `undefined` for every node: it parsed\n481\t\t * no string, so no coordinates exist to report. A node assembled from separate\n482\t\t * sources - a joined text run, a synthesized paragraph - reports `undefined` too,\n483\t\t * because no single region of the original covers it. Each call returns a fresh\n484\t\t * value rather than the stored one.\n485\t\t */\n486\t\tspan(node: MarkdownNode): MarkdownSpan | undefined\n487\t\t/** Rewrites the AST bottom-up (copy-on-write) and returns a new {@link MarkdownInterface}. */\n488\t\tmap(rewrite: MarkdownRewriteHandler): MarkdownInterface\n489\t\t/** Folds the AST depth-first, pre-order into an accumulator. */\n490\t\treduce<T>(callback: (accumulator: T, node: MarkdownNode) => T, initial: T): T\n491\t\t/** Runs a total catamorphism over the document using a {@link MarkdownHandlers} table. */\n492\t\tfold<T>(handlers: MarkdownHandlers<T>): T\n493\t\t/**\n494\t\t * A web-standard {@link ReadableStream} over the document's top-level block nodes\n495\t\t * (shallow, source order) - a lazy, pull-based, backpressure-respecting source. A\n496\t\t * fresh, independently-replayable stream every call; never mutates the document.\n497\t\t */"}]},"uuid":"e513fc9e-a79e-4549-ae48-0bc239c972ff","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:08:13.263Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"69a9aa25-760b-465d-83fb-531488b1f333","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}

The four constants below are this\n3\t// package's own, and are the only part a sibling package changes.\n4\t\n5\timport { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'\n6\timport {\n7\t\tcreateGuide,\n8\t\tcreateSource,\n9\t\tcreateSourceManager,\n10\t\tfenceImports,\n11\t\tfindMissing,\n12\t\tfindUnexampled,\n13\t\tfindUnlisted,\n14\t\tisExternalLink,\n15\t\tmissingSymbols,\n16\t\tparseManifest,\n17\t\tresolveLink,\n18\t\tsymbolKey,\n19\t} from '@orkestrel/guide'\n20\timport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'\n21\timport { requireValue } from '@orkestrel/test'\n22\timport { readInventory } from '@orkestrel/test/server'\n23\t\n24\t/** Every fence language this package's guides are allowed to use. */\n25\tconst FENCE_LANGUAGES = Object.freeze(['text', 'ts'])\n26\t/** The fence language whose blocks count as worked examples. */\n27\tconst EXAMPLE_LANGUAGE = 'ts'\n28\t/** Each import specifier this package's own guides may resolve against. */\n29\tconst MODULES = Object.freeze({ '@orkestrel/markdown': 'src/core', '@src/core': 'src/core' })\n30\t/**\n31\t * Declarations deliberately kept out of the barrel, as `symbolKey` strings.\n32\t *\n33\t * A class that one-class-per-file evicted from its single consumer cannot become a\n34\t * local, so it stays exported without being public. Naming it here is what makes that\n35\t * intentional rather than forgotten — and the second assertion below fails when a name\n36\t * here stops being stranded, so the list cannot rot.\n37\t */\n38\tconst INTERNAL: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([])\n39\t\n40\t/** Root-level files this package's guides link to. `readInventory` walks directories only. */\n41\tconst ROOT_FILES = Object.freeze(['AGENTS.md'])\n42\t\n43\tconst root = new URL('../', import.meta.url)\n44\tconst files: Record<string, string> = {\n45\t\t...readInventory(root, ['src', 'guides', 'tests'], { extensions: ['.ts', '.md'] }),\n46\t}\n47\tfor (const name of ROOT_FILES) files[name] = readFileSync(new URL(name, root), 'utf8')\n48\tconst manifest = parseManifest(\n49\t\trequireValue(files['guides/README.md'], 'Missing file: guides/README.md'),\n50\t\t'guides',\n51\t)\n52\tconst sources = createSourceManager({ files, modules: MODULES })\n53\t\n54\tit('manifest lists at least one guide', () => {\n55\t\texpect(manifest.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)\n56\t})\n57\t\n58\tfor (const entry of manifest) {\n59\t\tconst guide = createGuide(requireValue(files[entry.spec], `Missing file: ${entry.spec}`))\n60\t\tconst source = createSource({ files, module: entry.source })\n61\t\n62\t\tdescribe(`${entry.concept}`, () => {\n63\t\t\tit('uses only listed fence languages', () => {\n64\t\t\t\texpect(findUnlisted(guide.fences(), FENCE_LANGUAGES)).toEqual([])\n65\t\t\t})\n66\t\n67\t\t\tit('extracts a non-empty documented stranded = missingSymbols(source.exports(), source.surface())\n72\t\t\t\texpect(stranded.filter((key) => !INTERNAL.includes(key))).toEqual([])\n73\t\t\t})\n74\t\t\tit('names no symbol internal that the barrel already exports', () => {\n75\t\t\t\tconst stranded = missingSymbols(source.exports(), source.surface())\n76\t\t\t\texpect(INTERNAL.filter((key) => !stranded.includes(key))).toEqual([])\n77\t\t\t})\n78\t\t\tit('re-exports only direct declarations', () => {\n79\t\t\t\texpect(missingSymbols(source.surface(), source.exports())).toEqual([])\n80\t\t\t})\n81\t\t\tit('documents every barrel export', () => {\n82\t\t\t\texpect(missingSymbols(source.surface(), guide.surface())).toEqual([])\n83\t\t\t})\n84\t\t\tit('documents only barrel exports', () => {\n85\t\t\t\texpect(missingSymbols(guide.surface(), source.surface())).toEqual([])\n86\t\t\t})\n87\t\n88\t\t\tit('exposes no hidden module-scope declarations', () => {\n89\t\t\t\texpect(source.hidden().map(symbolKey)).toEqual([])\n90\t\t\t})\n91\t\n92\t\t\tfor (const group of guide.methods()) {\n93\t\t\t\tconst members = source.methods(group.interface)\n94\t\t\t\tconst entity = group.interface.replace(/Interface$/, '')\n95\t\t\t\tdescribe(`${group.interface}`, () => {\n96\t\t\t\t\tit('documents at least one method', () => {\n97\t\t\t\t\t\texpect(group.methods.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)\n98\t\t\t\t\t})\n99\t\t\t\t\tit('documents every interface method', () => {\n100\t\t\t\t\t\texpect(findMissing(members, group.methods)).toEqual([])\n101\t\t\t\t\t})\n102\t\t\t\t\tit('documents no phantom method', () => {\n103\t\t\t\t\t\texpect(findMissing(group.methods, members)).toEqual([])\n104\t\t\t\t\t})\n105\t\t\t\t\tit(`${entity} exposes no undocumented method`, () => {\n106\t\t\t\t\t\tconst extra =\n107\t\t\t\t\t\t\tentity === group.interface ? What `splitLines` returns per line, and what the block phase carries instead of a bare string.                                             |\n60:[Omitted long matching line]\n65:| `parseDocument`   | function | `(markdown: string) => MarkdownDocument`                                                                                             | Parses a markdown string into a `MarkdownDocument` AST through the block phase (`splitLines` + `parseBlocks`) — the document projection of `parseProvenance`. anything left over collects into a paragraph. `startsBlock` lets a new block interrupt a paragraph without a separating blank line.\n227:2. **Inline phase** — each block's raw text runs through `scanInline` (backslash escapes, code spans, links, images, emphasis, and the two-trailing-space hard break) via `parseInline`, then `coalesceText` merges adjacent text runs.\n229:[Omitted long matching line]\n240:- **Inline recursion** (`scanInline`, and the `depth` threaded through `scanLink` / `scanEmphasis`) — past the cap, the scan window is not scanned for markup at all; Each returns its parsed node and the index its syntax\n802\tended at, or `undefined` when the shape does not hold — the point at which the engine degrades the\n803\topening marker to literal text.\n804\t\n805\t```ts\n806\timport { scanEmphasis, scanInline, scanLink } from '@orkestrel/markdown'\n807\t\n808\tscanInline('a **b** c', 0, 9) // readonly InlineNode[] — text, emphasis, text\n809\t\n810\tconst link = scanLink('[docs](https://x.dev)', 0, 21)\n811\tlink?.node.href // 'https://x.dev'\n812\tlink?.end // 21\n813\t\n814\tconst emphasis = scanEmphasis('**bold**', 0, 8)\n815\temphasis?.node.strong // true\n816\temphasis?.end // 8\n817\t\n818\tscanLink('[unclosed', 0, 9) // undefined — degrades to a literal `[`\n819\t```\n820\t\n821\t`locateLink` and `locateEmphasis` are the same scans without the node construction: they return the\n822\tsyntax bounds alone, which is what the offset-bearing path needs to record a region before it builds\n823\tanything (§ [Source provenance](#source-provenance)).\n824\t\n825\t### Guide-parity extraction\n826\t\n827\t```ts\n828\timport { Markdown, isTableNode, flattenText } from '@orkestrel/markdown'\n829\t\n830\t// Extract every Surface-table first-column identifier from this very guide.\n831\tfunction extractSurfaceNames(source: string): readonly string[] {\n832\t\tconst markdown = new Markdown(source)\n833\t\tconst tables = markdown.filter(isTableNode) // readonly TableNode[] — narrowed, no cast needed\n834\t\treturn tables.flatMap((table) =>\n835\t\t\ttable.rows.map((row) => flattenText({ element: 'paragraph', children: row[0] ?? [] })),\n836\t\t)\n837\t}\n838\t```\n839\t"}]},"uuid":"9f9164ad-be89-454c-a682-bb4b0720a9d2","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:11:07.052Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a3bc3b47-c585-4a19-94d4-3213f801111f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}

THE HTML-to-markdown element mapping: which markdown node each HTML tag becomes, with every other element unwrapped to its children and every `UNSAFE_ELEMENTS` subtree dropped whole.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |\n110\t| `projectionToBlocks`     | function | `(projection: MarkdownProjection) => readonly BlockNode[]`                                                                                                      | Reads a projection as block content: a bare inline run becomes one paragraph, a blank run becomes nothing, and a cell or row that never reached a table unwraps.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |\n111\t| `projectionToInlines`    | function | `(projection: MarkdownProjection) => readonly InlineNode[]`                                                                                                     | Reads a projection as inline content: inline content passes through, block content flattens to one whitespace-collapsed text node.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |\n112\t| `walkNodes`              | function | `(node: MarkdownNode) => Generator<MarkdownNode>`                                                                                                               | Depth-first, pre-order, root-inclusive traversal — yields the node itself then its children. `Markdown.find` / `filter` / `reduce` / iteration all walk through this.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |\n113\t| `foldNode`               | function | `<T>(node: MarkdownNode, handlers: MarkdownHandlers<T>, depth: number) => T`                                                                                    | The total catamorphism `Markdown.fold` delegates to — children folded first (post-order), then the node's own handler runs with the already-folded children. The `table` handler is NOT a leaf: it receives one folded `T` per inline node, flattened across all cells (header cells first in column order, then body rows' cells in row-then-column order) — recover cell boundaries from `node.header[c].length` / `node.rows[r][c].length`. |\n114\t| `rewriteDocument`        | function | `(document: MarkdownDocument, rewrite: MarkdownRewriteHandler) => MarkdownDerivation<MarkdownDocument>`                                                         | The bottom-up (copy-on-write) rewrite `Markdown.map` delegates to — the document root is never itself passed to `rewrite`. Capped at `MAX_DEPTH`: a subtree at the cap passes through unchanged instead of recursing further.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |\n115\t| `flattenText`            | function | `(node: MarkdownNode) => string`                                                                                                                                | Concatenates the `value`/`code` content of every descendant text/code-span/code-block node, in walk order — a plain-text projection of an AST.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |\n116\t\n117\t### Shapers\n118\t"}]},"uuid":"d86b8d22-8ecb-4671-841f-c099b167a7e3","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:11:20.162Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"699dfb58-de05-4b28-8827-054643dd52d5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}

1),\n177\t\t\t\t),\n178\t\t\t\t'\\n',\n179\t\t\t)\n180\t\t\tconst node: BlockNode = {\n181\t\t\t\telement: 'paragraph',\n182\t\t\t\tchildren: coalesceText(scanInlineSource(source, 0, source.text.length, spans), spans),\n183\t\t\t}\n184\t\t\tconst region = joinSources(lines.slice(start, index), '\\n')\n185\t\t\tconst span = projectSpan(region, 0, region.text.length)\n186\t\t\tif (span !== undefined) spans.set(node, span)\n187\t\t\tblocks.push(node)\n188\t\t}\n189\t\treturn blocks\n190\t}\n191\t\n192\t/**\n193\t * Parses a markdown string into a typed {@link MarkdownDocument} AST via the\n194\t * block phase.\n195\t *\n196\t * @param markdown - The markdown source to parse.\n197\t * @returns The parsed document.\n198\t */\n199\texport function parseDocument(markdown: string): MarkdownDocument {\n200\t\tconst [document] = parseProvenance(markdown)\n201\t\treturn document\n202\t}\n203\t\n204\t/**\n205\t * Parses a markdown string into a document and its original-source spans.\n206\t *\n207\t * @param markdown - The markdown source to parse.\n208\t * @returns The parsed document and its node-identity span map.\n209\t */\n210\texport function parseProvenance(markdown: string): MarkdownParseResult {\n211\t\tconst spans = new Map<MarkdownNode, MarkdownSpan>()\n212\t\tconst document: MarkdownDocument = {\n213\t\t\telement: 'document',\n214\t\t\tchildren: parseBlocks(splitLines(markdown), 0, spans, markdown.length),\n215\t\t}\n216\t\tspans.set(document, { start: 0, end: markdown.length })\n217\t\treturn [document, spans]\n218\t}\n219\t\n220\t/**\n221\t * Parses inline markdown text (emphasis, code spans, links, images, and hard\n222\t * breaks) into inline AST nodes, coalescing adjacent text runs.\n223\t *\n224\t * @param text - The inline markdown text to parse.\n225\t * @returns The parsed inline nodes.\n226\t */\n227\texport function parseInline(text: string): readonly InlineNode[] {\n228\t\treturn coalesceText(scanInline(text, 0, text.length))\n229\t}\n230\t"}]},"uuid":"9d9a2412-e39a-4a5f-a3e2-ff8dc7b4e554","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:12:05.484Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"193853de-1010-4d4c-857b-762c54fe742d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}

undefined : { start: span.start, end: span.end }\n91-\t}\n92-\n--\n141-\t */\n142-\tmap(rewrite: MarkdownRewriteHandler): MarkdownInterface {\n143-\t\tconst [document, derivations] = rewriteDocument(this.#document, rewrite)\n144:\t\treturn this.#derive(document, derivations)\n145-\t}\n146-\n147-\t/** Folds the AST depth-first, pre-order into an accumulator. */\n--\n206-\t// first terminates the chain on the node's own region instead of following that entry\n207-\t// onto a foreign one - both when the node is being resolved and when a later hop\n208-\t// reaches it as someone else's source.\n209:\t#derive(\n210-\t\tdocument: MarkdownDocument,\n211-\t\tderivations: ReadonlyMap<MarkdownNode, MarkdownNode | undefined>,\n212-\t): Markdown {\n--\n217-\t\t\tlet source: MarkdownNode | undefined = node\n218-\t\t\twhile (source !== undefined && !visited.has(source)) {\n219-\t\t\t\tvisited.add(source)\n220:\t\t\t\tconst span = this.#spans.get(source)\n221-\t\t\t\tif (span !== undefined) {\n222:\t\t\t\t\tderived.#spans.set(node, span)\n223-\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n224-\t\t\t\t}\n225-\t\t\t\tsource = derivations.get(source)"}]},"uuid":"6bac806f-bf7b-4f98-8729-10a146fde651","timestamp":"2026-08-26T08:13:53.099Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"29f716f9-d63f-49a6-b702-b8e22feaf605","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","cwd":"/home/user/markdown","sessionId":"e44afe43-d783-57c4-9b94-e1b722b0b4a2","version":"2.1.246","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"zany-launching-leaf"}
